Sub A_0()
'Range("A1:A2").RemoveDuplicates 1

Dim srr As String
srr = ""
For i = 1 To 5
srr = srr + "a=" + Str(100 + i) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Next i

Dim myData As New DataObject
'myData.SetText (Cells(6, 14))
myData.SetText srr
'Перемещаем текст из DataObject в буфер обмена
myData.PutInClipboard
'Проверяем содержимое буфера обмена
ActiveSheet.Paste Range("A1:A50")
End Sub

Sub K_Vol_uni()
Kv = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(17, 57).Value
xK = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(20, 57).Value
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
aa = Worksheets.Count
'проверка имен параметров связи
For i = 1 To aa
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then
Worksheets(bb).Cells(7, 4).Value = Kv
End If
End If
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End Sub

Sub prin_KMS_Rlin()

answer = MsgBox("Вы стираете все расчётные данные по гидравлике и присваиваете константы!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ?", vbQuestion + vbYesNo + vbDefaultButton2, "Присвоение престенов для гидравлики!")

If answer = vbYes Then


pKMS = Cells(6, 72).Value
pRlin = Cells(6, 71).Value
aa = Worksheets.Count
For i = 3 To aa
For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value) > 1 Then
Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 12).Value = pRlin
Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 13).Value = pKMS
End If
Next j
Next i
End If
End Sub


Sub Massh_KMS_Rlin()
kmm = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(11, 69).Value
dop_t = ""
prov = 1
If IsNumeric(kmm) = False Then prov = 0
If kmm < 0.05 Then prov = 0
If kmm > 10.05 Then prov = 0
If prov = 0 Then Worksheets("CODE").Cells(11, 69).Value = 1
If prov = 0 Then dop_t = "Проведены испраления значения коэффициента масштаба!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
answer = MsgBox(dop_t + "Вы масштабируете все расчётные данные по гидравлике!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ?", vbQuestion + vbYesNo + vbDefaultButton2, "Присвоение престенов для гидравлики!")

If answer = vbYes Then



    ufProgress.Show (0)
aa = Worksheets.Count
For i = 3 To aa
For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
ima_l = Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value
If Len(ima_l) > 1 Then
r_lin = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 12).Value
k_ms = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 13).Value
Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 12).Value = r_lin * kmm
Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 13).Value = k_ms * kmm
End If
Next j
    Call PrPro(100 * (i) / (aa + 0.0000000001), "Масштабирование коэффициентов гидравлики связей!")
Next i

Worksheets("CODE").Cells(11, 69).Value = 1
Unload ufProgress
End If

End Sub

Sub load_all_k() ' присвоить всем к одно значение
i = 63
Do
If Len(Cells(i, 49).Value) > 5 Then
For j0 = 1 To 19
If Len(Cells(i, 97 + 3 * j0).Value) > 0 Then
Cells(i, 97 + 3 * j0).Value = Cells(56, 102).Value
End If
Next j0
End If
i = i + 1
Loop Until Cells(i, 54).Value = ""

End Sub

Sub Ur_feed()
aa1 = 0
aa2 = 0
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Cells(i, 7).Value) > 1 Then
If (Cells(i + 4, 26)) = 1 Then
aa1 = aa1 + Val(Cells(i + 3, 26))
End If
If (Cells(i + 4, 26)) = -1 Then
aa2 = aa2 + Val(Cells(i + 3, 26))
End If
End If
Next i
If aa1 = 0 Then
MsgBox ("Из описываемого параметра не выходит ни одного потока")
End If
If aa2 = 0 Then
MsgBox ("В описываемый параметр не входит ни один поток")
End If
k = 1
If aa1 * aa2 > 0 Then
k = aa1 / aa2
End If
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Cells(i, 7).Value) > 1 Then
If (Cells(i + 4, 26)) = 1 Then
Cells(i + 3, 26).Value = Format(1000 * Val(Cells(i + 3, 26)) / k, 0#) / 1000
End If
End If
Next i

End Sub

End Sub

Sub Gen_Ways()
'If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Worksheets("CODE").Activate
Call GEN_USL_x
Call Gen_perech
Call Gen_bibU
tipPe = Cells(56, 289).Value
If tipPe = 1 Then Exit Sub
Range(Cells(63, 287), Cells(1563, 13000)).ClearContents
aa = Worksheets.Count
way_x = 0
i = 63
Do
i = i + 1
Loop While Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 285).Value < 2
chi = i - 63

i = 63
Do
i = i + 1
Loop While Len(Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 286).Value) > 1
cho = i - 63

Dim i_ur(50) As Single ' последнй номер просмотренный на уровне при поиске
Dim u_ur(50) As String ' связь
Dim uist(50) As String ' набор источников цепи для каждого уровня

ind_o = 0

i_ur(iid) = 0
uist(iid) = ""
Next iid

stb = 0

If i = 11 Then
aa = aa
End If

uro = 1
j = 0

Do
i_ur(uro + 1) = 0
For j = i_ur(uro) + 1 To cho

str_x = 0

old_r = i_ur(uro - 2)

End If

a1 = a1 + NM_z(i_ur(uiu)) + "-->"

str_x = 1
End If

    For sv_i = 1 To 3
        j_len = Len(SV_xy(sv_i, i_ur(uro - 1), j))
        If chSV_i(j) > 12 Then j_len = 0
        If j_len > 0 Then
        df = SV_xy(sv_i, i_ur(uro - 1), j)

        ind_i = 0
        ind_o = 1
        i_ur(uro) = j
        u_ur(uro) = SV_xy(sv_i, i_ur(uro - 1), j)
            If Tip_z(j) < 2 Then ' значит надо записать путь
                stb = stb + 1

                uist(uro) = NM_z(i)
                    For iu = 0 To uro
                    Ist_y(way_x, iu) = i_ur(iu)
                    If iu > 0 Then
                    uist(uro) = uist(uro) + "*|*" + NM_z(i_ur(iu))
                    USL_y(way_x, iu) = u_ur(iu)
                    If tipPe = 3 Then Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i + 62, stb + 286).Value = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i + 62, stb + 286).Value + "-(" + u_ur(iu) + ")-" + NM_z(i_ur(iu))
                    End If
                    Next iu
                    If tipPe = 2 Then Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i + 62, stb + 286).Value = uist(uro)
                ind_i = 1
                a = a
            End If
        shag = shag + 1
        
        'If i = 82 Then
    Cells(53, 295).Value = way_x

    Cells(55, 295).Value = i
    Cells(56, 295).Value = NM_z(i)

    Cells(58, 295).Value = NM_z(j)
    Cells(59, 295).Value = Tip_z(j)

    Cells(54, 298).Value = ind_i

     a = a

        End If

'piH2_1 -(1)-pipH2_1-(1*(k3_2))-H2_1_2_in-(1*(k3_1))-isp1_1

Sub Gen_bibU()
aa = Worksheets.Count
Worksheets("CODE").Range(Cells(63, 287), Cells(1563, 306)).ClearContents

For svya = 1 To 3
For i = 1 To 1000
chSV_i(i) = 0
For li = 1 To 1000
SV_xy(svya, i, li) = ""
Next li, i, svya

' генерируем имена начала и звеньев, а также связи
i = 63 ' имена и типы
Do
NM_z(i - 62) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 286).Value
Tip_z(i - 62) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 285).Value
i = i + 1
Loop While Len(Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 286).Value) > 1
chi = i - 63

For i = 1 To chi ' для безусловных
sh = 0
stoo = 287
If Tip_z(i) = 1 Then
    For li = i To chi
    If Tip_z(li) = 1 Then
    Else
    svya = 0
        For isi = 1 To 19
        If Worksheets(NM_z(li)).Cells((isi - 1) * 7 + 5, 7).Value = NM_z(i) Then
        svya = svya + 1
        SV_xy(svya, i, li) = Worksheets(NM_z(li)).Cells((isi - 1) * 7 + 9, 16).Value
        sh = sh + 1
        Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, stoo).Value = NM_z(li) + "=" + SV_xy(svya, i, li)
        stoo = stoo + 1
        End If
        Next isi
    End If
    Next li

Else ' для условных
  svya = 1
        For isi = 1 To 19
        
            ist_k = Worksheets(NM_z(i)).Cells((isi - 1) * 7 + 5, 7).Value
            
            If Len(ist_k) > 1 Then
                io = 62
                Do
                io = io + 1
                Loop Until ist_k = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(io, 286).Value
            io = io - 62
            If Len(SV_xy(svya, i, li)) > 0 Then svya = svya + 1
            SV_xy(svya, i, io) = Worksheets(NM_z(i)).Cells((isi - 1) * 7 + 9, 16).Value
            sh = sh + 1
            Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, stoo).Value = ist_k + "=" + SV_xy(svya, i, io)
            stoo = stoo + 1
           End If
           
        Next isi
    
    
End If
chSV_i(i) = sh
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, 283).Value = sh
'If sh > 10 Then Tip_z(i) = 2
Next i
'MsgBox ("Done!")

End Sub

Sub Gen_perech()
i = 63
Range(Cells(63, 285), Cells(1563, 386)).ClearContents
Do
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 54).Interior.Color = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62, 48).Interior.Color Then
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 286).Value = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 54).Value
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 285).Value = 1
End If
i = i + 1
Loop While Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 54).Interior.Color = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62, 48).Interior.Color

aa = Worksheets.Count
For j = 3 To aa
If Worksheets(j).Cells(9, 3).Value = 1 Then
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 286).Value = Worksheets(j).Cells(4, 4).Value
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 285).Value = 0
i = i + 1
End If
Next j

For j = 3 To aa
If Worksheets(j).Cells(9, 3).Value = 0 Then
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 286).Value = Worksheets(j).Cells(4, 4).Value
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, 285).Value = 2
i = i + 1
End If
Next j

End Sub

Sub GEN_USL_x()
aa = Worksheets.Count
chsv = 0
For liss = 3 To aa
    For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
        USL_x = "1"
        Worksheets(liss).Cells(i + 4, 16).Value = ""
        If Len(Worksheets(liss).Cells(i, 7).Value) > 1 Then
            For j = 10 To 26 Step 2
                k1 = (Worksheets(liss).Cells(i, j).Value)
                k2 = (Worksheets(liss).Cells(i + 1, j).Value)
                k3 = (Worksheets(liss).Cells(i + 2, j).Value)
                ind_k = Len(k1) + Len(k2) + Len(k3)
                kk1 = "0"
                If Len(k1) > 0 Then kk1 = k1
                kk2 = ""
                If Len(k2) > 0 Then kk2 = "+" + k2
                kk3 = ""
                If Len(k3) > 0 Then kk3 = "+" + k3
                If ind_k > 0 Then
                USL_x = USL_x + "*(" + kk1 + kk2 + kk3 + ")"
                chsv = chsv + 1
                End If
            Next j
            If IsNumeric(USL_x) = True Then USL_x = Str(USL_x) + "*()"
            Worksheets(liss).Cells(i + 4, 16).Value = USL_x
        End If
    Next i
    Call PrPro(100 * (liss - 2) / (aa - 2), "Библиотека. Условия связи штуцеров!")
Next liss
chsv = Format(100 * chsv / (Worksheets.Count - 2), 0#) / 100
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(53, 12).Value = chsv
End Sub



Function Zap_dp(nomber1 As Single, nomber2 As Single)
n1 = nomber1
n2 = nomber2
NM1 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(n1 + 62, 54).Value
nm2 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(n2 + 62, 54).Value
roh = Replace("ro" + nm2 + NM1, " ", "")
ro1 = Replace("ro" + NM1, " ", "")
ro2 = Replace("ro" + nm2, " ", "")

shn1 = 0
shn2 = 0
tip2 = 0
For i = 1 To 19
If Worksheets(NM1).Cells(7 * i - 2, 7).Value = nm2 Then
shn1 = i
tip2 = Worksheets(NM1).Cells(7 * i + 1, 7).Value
End If
Next i
If tip2 = 2 Then
For i = 1 To 19
If Worksheets(nm2).Cells(7 * i - 2, 7).Value = NM1 Then shn2 = i
Next i
End If


out_n = ""
out_n = out_n + "h001=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
out_n = out_n + "if " + Replace("(L_" + NM1, " ", "") + "> LS_r[" + Str(n1) + "," + Str(shn1) + "]) h001=(" + Replace("(L_" + NM1, " ", "") + "- LS_r[" + Str(n1) + "," + Str(shn1) + "])/100*ISTO_H[" + Str(n1) + "];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
out_n = out_n + "h002=ISTO_HN[" + Str(n1) + "]+LS_r[" + Str(n1) + "," + Str(shn1) + "]/100*ISTO_H[" + Str(n1) + "];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

If tip2 = 1 Then ' безусловный источник
out_n = out_n + "h003=0;"
out_n = out_n + "h004=0;"
Else 'условный источник
out_n = out_n + "h003=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
out_n = out_n + "if " + Replace("(L_" + nm2, " ", "") + "> LS_r[" + Str(n2) + "," + Str(shn2) + "]) h003=(" + Replace("(L_" + nm2, " ", "") + "- LS_r[" + Str(n2) + "," + Str(shn2) + "])/100*ISTO_H[" + Str(n2) + "];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
out_n = out_n + "h004=ISTO_HN[" + Str(n2) + "]+LS_r[" + Str(n2) + "," + Str(shn2) + "]/100*ISTO_H[" + Str(n2) + "];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If
out_n = out_n + "dPL_r[" + Str(n1) + "," + Str(shn1) + "]=(" + roh + "*(h002-h004)+" + ro1 + "*h001-" + ro2 + "*h003)/10.33;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Zap_dp = out_n
End Function

Sub test11()
'Cells(18, 1).Value = Zap_dp((100), (101))

For i = 1 To 200
For j = 1 To 50
prov = 1
If i = 4 Then
If j = 1 Then prov = 0
End If
If prov = 1 Then
If Cells(i, j).Interior.Color = Cells(4, 1).Interior.Color Then
Cells(i, j).Interior.Color = Cells(4, 2).Interior.Color
End If
End If
Next j
Next i
End Sub

Sub found_sovp() ' проставляем задвижки во всех смежных источниках как тут в образце
ActiveWorkbook.ActiveSheet.Name = Cells(4, 4).Value
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(12, 166).Value = 0 ' индикатор требования сбора библиотеки
' проверяем на типы в ячейках
oso = ""
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
For j = 9 To 25 Step 2
For jj = 1 To 3
If Len(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j).Value) > 0 Then
jko = 0
If Cells(i - 1 + jj, j).Value = "b" Then jko = 1
If Cells(i - 1 + jj, j).Value = "f" Then jko = 1
If jko = 0 Then
If IsNumeric(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j).Value) = False Then oso = oso + " Cвязь с источником " + Cells(i, 7).Value + " прописана с ошибкой, в г.с. арматуры №" + Str((j - 7) / 2) + " указано текстовое значение <" + Cells(i - 1 + jj, j).Value + "> вместо числового! Линия:" + Str(jj) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If
End If

If Len(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j + 1).Value) > 0 Then
If IsNumeric(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j + 1).Value) = True Then uip = Str(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j + 1).Value)
If IsNumeric(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j + 1).Value) = False Then uip = Cells(i - 1 + jj, j + 1).Value
If IsNumeric(Left(Cells(i - 1 + jj, j + 1).Value, 1)) = True Then oso = oso + " Cвязь с источником " + Cells(i, 7).Value + " прописана с ошибкой, в имени арматуры №" + Str((j - 7) / 2) + " указано числовое значение <" + uip + "> вместо текстового! Линия:" + Str(jj) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If


Next jj

Next j
Next i

If Len(oso) > 1 Then
MsgBox (oso)
Exit Sub
End If

'If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Dim nex(20) As Single
nx = 0
Dim myDOUB0 As New DataObject
Dim sov(20) As Single
ima = Cells(4, 4).Value
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
    tst_n = 0
    For Fo = 1 To 20
    If i = nex(nx) Then tst_n = 1
    Next Fo
    If tst_n = 0 Then ' tst_n
    ima2 = Cells(i, 7).Value
    If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value = 2 Then
    If Len(Cells(i, 7).Value) > 2 Then 'нашли зависимый источник, ищем вдург несколько раз он упомянается
    
    net_lista = 1 ' создаем доп лист если не создан еще
    For jj = 3 To Worksheets.Count
    If ima2 = Worksheets(jj).Name Then net_lista = 0
    Next jj
    If net_lista = 1 Then
    plus_sheet
    dd = "EX (2)"
    dd = Worksheets("EX").Cells(1, 1).Value
    Worksheets("EX").Cells(1, 1).Value = "1"
    Worksheets(ima).Activate
    Worksheets(dd).Name = ima2
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(4, 4).Value = ima2
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(5, 7).Value = ima
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(5 + 3, 7).Value = 2
    End If
    
    
    
    povt = 1
    sov(povt) = i
    For j = i + 7 To 131
    If Cells(j, 7).Value = ima2 Then
    povt = povt + 1
    sov(povt) = j
    nx = nx + 1
    nex(nx) = j
    End If
    Next j
    i0 = 5
    povt2 = 0
    Do
    If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0, 7) = ima Then
    povt2 = povt2 + 1

    If sov(povt2) = 0 Then
    Worksheets(ima2).Activate
    Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(i0, 9), Cells(i0 + 3, 28)).ClearContents
    Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(i0, 7), Cells(i0 + 2, 7)).ClearContents
    Else
    Worksheets(ima).Range(Cells(sov(povt2), 9), Cells(sov(povt2) + 3, 28)).Copy
    myDOUB0.GetFromClipboard
    Worksheets(ima2).Activate
    nom_p = Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0 + 3, 9).Value
    Worksheets(ima2).Paste Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(i0, 9), Cells(i0 + 3, 28))
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0 + 3, 9).Value = nom_p
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0 + 6, 5).Value = Worksheets(ima).Cells(sov(povt2) + 6, 5).Value
    
    
    'Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(I0, 9), Cells(I0 + 2, 28)).Replace "b", "b1", 1
    'Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(I0, 9), Cells(I0 + 2, 28)).Replace "f", "b", 1
    'Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(I0, 9), Cells(I0 + 2, 28)).Replace "b1", "f", 1
    
    
    End If
    Application.CutCopyMode = False
    Worksheets(ima).Activate
    End If
    i0 = i0 + 7
    Loop Until i0 > 131
    
    fpov = povt2
    If povt2 < povt Then 'если нет источника в списке
    For povi = 1 To povt - povt2
    fpov = fpov + 1
    i0 = -2
    Do
    i0 = i0 + 7
    Loop Until Len(Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0, 7).Value) < 2
    
    If i0 > 124 Then
    MsgBox ("У источника " + ima2 + " число допустимых контактов - 19. При этом установлен 18-ый контакт. Источник близок к переполнению по связям и следующий источник не сможет быть добавлен! В случае необходимости - создайте клон " + ima2)
    End If
    
    If i0 > 131 Then
    MsgBox ("У источника " + ima2 + " превышено число допустимых контактов - 19." + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "Источник " + ima1 + " не может быть добавлен! Создайте клон " + ima2)
    Else
    If sov(fpov) = 0 Then
    Worksheets(ima2).Activate
    Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(i0, 9), Cells(i0 + 3, 28)).ClearContents
    Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(i0, 7), Cells(i0 + 2, 7)).ClearContents
    Else
    Worksheets(ima).Activate
    Worksheets(ima).Range(Cells(sov(fpov), 9), Cells(sov(fpov) + 3, 28)).Copy
    myDOUB0.GetFromClipboard
    Worksheets(ima2).Activate
    nom_p = Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0 + 3, 9).Value
    Worksheets(ima2).Paste Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(i0, 9), Cells(i0 + 3, 28))
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0 + 3, 9).Value = nom_p
    Worksheets(ima2).Cells(i0, 7).Value = ima
    
    'Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(I0, 9), Cells(I0 + 2, 28)).Replace "b", "b1", 1
    'Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(I0, 9), Cells(I0 + 2, 28)).Replace "f", "b", 1
    'Worksheets(ima2).Range(Cells(I0, 9), Cells(I0 + 2, 28)).Replace "b1", "f", 1
    End If
    End If
    Next povi
    End If
    
    
    End If
    End If
    End If ' tst_n
    Worksheets(ima).Activate
Next i


' меняем b/f
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
    If Worksheets(ima).Cells(i + 3, 7).Value = 2 Then
        ima2 = Worksheets(ima).Cells(i, 7).Value
        If Len(ima2) > 1 Then
            For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, 7).Value = ima Then 'нашли ответную связь на втором листе
            Worksheets(ima2).Activate
            For j2 = 9 To 27 Step 2
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, j2).Value = "f" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, j2).Value = "b1"
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, j2).Value = "b" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, j2).Value = "f"
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, j2).Value = "b1" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j, j2).Value = "b"
            
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 1, j2).Value = "f" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 1, j2).Value = "b1"
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 1, j2).Value = "b" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 1, j2).Value = "f"
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 1, j2).Value = "b1" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 1, j2).Value = "b"
            
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 2, j2).Value = "f" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 2, j2).Value = "b1"
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 2, j2).Value = "b" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 2, j2).Value = "f"
            If Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 2, j2).Value = "b1" Then Worksheets(ima2).Cells(j + 2, j2).Value = "b"
            Next j2
            End If
            Next j
        End If
    End If
Next i

Worksheets(ima).Activate
'Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End Sub

Sub all_GS()

kofzad = 1000 ' коэффициент для задвижек
lamda = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(8, 67).Value ' коэффициент трения трубопроводов
kp_ar = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(29, 60).Value ' коэффициент корректировки проходимости арматур

' считаем число кранов
i = 0
b = 0
Do
a = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(63 + i, 57).Value
If a = "" Then
b = 1
Else
i = i + 1
End If
Loop While b = 0
chK = i


' упорядочиваем имена кранов по длине их имени
Dim lenKR(5500) As Single
Dim imaKR(5500) As String
Dim imaKR_2(5500) As String
Dim nom_kr(5500) As Single
Dim nom_kr_2(5500) As Single
Dim tip_o(5500) As Single
Dim tip_o_2(5500) As Single
Dim otkr_o(5500) As Single
Dim otkr_o_2(5500) As Single

Dim proh_o(5500) As Single
Dim proh_o_2(5500) As Single

For i = 1 To 5500
imaKR(i) = ""
imaKR_2(i) = ""
lenKR(i) = 0
nom_kr(i) = 0
nom_kr_2(i) = 0
tip_o(i) = 0
tip_o_2(i) = 0
otkr_o(i) = 0
otkr_o_2(i) = 0
proh_o(i) = 0
proh_o_2(i) = 0
Next i

lon_00 = 0
For i = 1 To chK ' идем по кранам
imaKR(i) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, 57).Value
nom_kr(i) = 62 + i
lenKR(i) = Len(imaKR(i))
If lenKR(i) > lon_00 Then lon_00 = lenKR(i)
tip_o(i) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, 280).Value
otkr_o(i) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, 281).Value
proh_o(i) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(62 + i, 282).Value
Next i


sch_13 = 0
For LL = lon_00 To 0 Step -1

For ii = 1 To chK
If LL = lenKR(ii) Then
sch_13 = sch_13 + 1

imaKR_2(sch_13) = imaKR(ii)
nom_kr_2(sch_13) = nom_kr(ii)
tip_o_2(sch_13) = tip_o(ii)
otkr_o_2(sch_13) = otkr_o(ii)
proh_o_2(sch_13) = proh_o(ii)
End If
Next ii
Next LL






For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
dd = Cells(i + 3, 10).Value
LL = Cells(i + 3, 11).Value
kz = dd / 1000
dd = dd / 1000 + 0.00001
kr = lamda * ((LL / dd) ^ (1))

    If Len(Cells(i, 7).Value) > 1 Then '''''''''''''''''(1)
    
    Cells(i + 3, 12).Value = Format(kr * 1000, 0#) / 1000
    For j = 9 To 27 Step 2
        For n = 1 To 3
        kz = 1
        st = 0
        kof = Cells(i - 1 + n, j).Value
        ind = 0
        kof1 = Cells(i - 1 + n, j + 1).Value
            If Len(kof1) > 1 Then
            kof1 = Replace(kof1, " ", "")
            ind = 2
            
            cr = 0
            stop_0 = 0
                Do
                cr = cr + 1
                
                If cr > chK Then stop_0 = 1
                
                    If InStr(kof1, imaKR_2(cr)) > 0 Then
                    
                        kof1 = Replace(kof1, imaKR_2(cr), "")
                        a1 = Len(kof1)
                        a2 = a1
                        a3 = a1
                        kof01 = kof1
                        kof02 = kof1
                        If kz = 1 Then
                        a1 = 100000
                        a3 = a1
                        ch = 1
                        Else
                        kof01 = Replace(kof1, "*", "")
                        kof02 = Replace(kof1, "+", "")
                        a2 = Len(kof01)
                        a3 = Len(kof02)
                        ch = ch + 1
                        End If
                        
                        'stop_0 = 2
                        
                        If a1 > a2 Then
                        st = st + 1
                        reg = 1
                        If otkr_o_2(cr) = 1 Then
                        reg = 0.2
                        End If
                        kz = ((kz ^ (st - 1)) * (reg * tip_o_2(cr) / kofzad / 1.5) ^ 1) ^ (1 / st)
                         
                         
                         If ch > 1 Then
                         'находим первый умножить и удаляем его
                         umn = InStr(kof1, "*")
                         kof_l = Left(kof1, umn - 1)
                         kof_r = Right(kof1, Len(kof1) - umn)
                         kof1 = kof_l + kof_r
                         End If
                        aa1 = aa1
                        End If
                        
                        If a1 > a3 Then
                        kz = ((ch - 1) * kz + (tip_o_2(cr) / kofzad / 1.5) ^ 1) / ch
                         If otkr_o_2(cr) = 1 Then kz = kz * (ch - 1 + 1 / 5) / ch
                         If ch > 1 Then
                         umn = InStr(kof1, "+")
                         kof_l = Left(kof1, umn - 1)
                         kof_r = Right(kof1, Len(kof1) - umn)
                         kof1 = kof_l + kof_r
                         End If
                        aa1 = aa1
                        End If
                        kz = kz * kp_ar
                      kz = kz * (proh_o_2(cr) + 1)
                       
                    End If
                Loop While stop_0 = 0
            
            
            End If
            
        If kof = "b" Then ind = ind + 3
        If kof = "f" Then ind = ind + 3
        If kz < 1 / 100000 Then kz = 1 / 100000
        If ind = 2 Then Cells(i - 1 + n, j).Value = Format(kz * 100000, 0#) / 100000
        Next n
    Next j
    
    Else
    Cells(i + 3, 10).Value = ""
    Cells(i + 3, 11).Value = ""
    Cells(i + 3, 12).Value = ""
    Cells(i + 3, 13).Value = ""
    Cells(i + 3, 14).Value = ""

    End If '''''''''''''''''(1)

Next i
Call V_ra
'Call found_sovp
End Sub

Sub V_ra() ' расчёт объема системы
If Cells(9, 3).Value = 0 Then
    v = 0
    For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
    dd = Cells(i + 3, 10).Value / 1000
    LL = Cells(i + 3, 11).Value / 2 ' учет того что в объем системы берем половину линии до следующего источника
    If Len(Cells(i, 7).Value) > 1 Then
    v = v + dd * dd * 3.14159 / 4 * LL
    End If
    Next i
    Cells(7, 4).Value = Format(v * 1000, 0#) / 1000
End If
End Sub

Sub i1gs()
For i = 5 To 7
For j = 10 To 28 Step 2
Cells(i, j).Value = Replace(Cells(i, j).Value, " ", "")
If Cells(i, j).Value = "" Then
Cells(i, j - 1).Value = ""
Else
Cells(i, j - 1).Value = 1
End If
Next j
Next i
End Sub

For i = 68 + 7 * 8 To 70 + 7 * 8
For j = 10 To 28 Step 2
Cells(i, j).Value = Replace(Cells(i, j).Value, " ", "")
If Cells(i, j).Value = "" Then
Cells(i, j - 1).Value = ""
Else
Cells(i, j - 1).Value = 1
End If
Next j
Next i
End Sub

Sub Dell18()
Range(Cells(68 + 7 * 8, 9), Cells(70 + 7 * 8, 28)).Value = ""
End Sub

Sub i19gs()
For i = 68 + 7 * 9 To 70 + 7 * 9
For j = 10 To 28 Step 2
Cells(i, j).Value = Replace(Cells(i, j).Value, " ", "")
If Cells(i, j).Value = "" Then
Cells(i, j - 1).Value = ""
Else
Cells(i, j - 1).Value = 1
End If
Next j
Next i
End Sub

Sub Dell19()
Range(Cells(68 + 7 * 9, 9), Cells(70 + 7 * 9, 28)).Value = ""
End Sub

Function Pm100(i0 As Single, Name_1 As String, Name_2 As String) '*dell
ima1 = Name_1
ima2 = Name_2
i = i0

isi = 0
st = 0
For i3 = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Cells(i3, 7).Value) > 1 Then
If st = 0 Then isi = isi + 1
If i3 > i0 - 1 Then st = 1
End If
Next i3

' определяем тип OI2=1/2
oi2 = 0
If Worksheets(Name_1).Cells(i0 + 3, 7).Value > 1 Then
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(9, 3).Value = 1 Then oi2 = 1


For i3 = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3, 7).Value) > 1 Then
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3 + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then oi2 = 1
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3 + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then oi2 = 1
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3 + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then oi2 = 1
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3 + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then oi2 = 1
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3 + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then oi2 = 1
If Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(i3 + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then oi2 = 1
End If
Next i3
End If



'If oi2 = 1 Then MsgBox ("прилегающий источник " + Name_2 + " является источником OI")

nOSN = Cells(4, 3).Value
nTEK = Cells(i0 + 3, 9).Value

Hsis = "0"
Hsis1 = Str(Worksheets(ima1).Cells(9, 4).Value)
If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value = 2 Then
Hsis2 = Str(Worksheets(ima2).Cells(9, 4).Value)
End If
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(U_r[" + Str(Cells(i, 6).Value) + "]>0.001)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(U_ri[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str(isi) + "]>0.001)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

'// это тупик функция
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value - oi2 = 2 Then

PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Gtc_fm + "<100*e27)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Gtc_fm1 + ">100*e27) U_r[" + Str(Cells(i, 6).Value) + "]=0.001;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Gtc_fm1 + ">100*e27) U_ri[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str(Cells(i, 6).Value) + "]=0.001;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa3=T_fff;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if (ISTO_ieg[" + Str(Worksheets(ima2).Cells(4, 3).Value) + "]*0.5+ISTO_ieg[" + Str(Worksheets(ima1).Cells(4, 3).Value) + "]*0.5>0.3) aa3=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Gtc_fm1 + ">100*e27) U_ri[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str(isi) + "]=U_ri[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str(isi) + "]/aa3;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "}" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

End If
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Replace("p" + ima2, " ", "") + "<0)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa03=aa03+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa05=aa05+" + Replace("p" + ima2, " ", "") + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "}" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa04=aa04+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa06=aa06+" + Replace("p" + ima2, " ", "") + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

Function Pm200(i0 As Single, Name_1 As String, Name_2 As String) '*dell
ima1 = Name_1
ima2 = Name_2
i = i0
isi = 0
st = 0
For i3 = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Cells(i3, 7).Value) > 1 Then
If st = 0 Then isi = isi + 1
If i3 > i0 - 1 Then st = 1
End If
Next i3
' определяем тип OI2=1/2
oi2 = 0
If Worksheets(Name_1).Cells(i0 + 3, 7).Value > 1 Then
oi2 = Worksheets(Name_2).Cells(11, 3).Value
End If

' определяем не насос ли

nOSN = Cells(4, 3).Value
nTEK = Cells(i0 + 3, 9).Value
Hsis = "0"
Hsis1 = Str(Worksheets(ima1).Cells(9, 4).Value)
If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value = 2 Then
Hsis2 = Str(Worksheets(ima2).Cells(9, 4).Value)
End If
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(U_ri[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str(isi) + "]>0.001)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
'// это тупик функция
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Gtc_fm = "GTc" + ima2 + "*" + "Fm" + ima2
Gtc_fm1 = "GTc" + ima1 + "*" + "Fm" + ima1

If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value - oi2 = 2 Then
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Gtc_fm + "<100*e27)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa3=T_fff;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if (ISTO_ieg[" + Str(Worksheets(ima2).Cells(4, 3).Value) + "]*0.5+ISTO_ieg[" + Str(Worksheets(ima1).Cells(4, 3).Value) + "]*0.5>0.3) aa3=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "}" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If
'zap = "100*Math.Abs(GTc" + ima2 + "+Fm" + ima2 + "-" + "GTc" + ima1 + "-Fm" + ima1 + ")/(GTc" + ima1 + "+Fm" + ima1 + "+e27)+e27"
ZAP = "100*Math.Abs(GTc" + ima2 + "+Fm" + ima2 + "-" + "GTc" + ima1 + "-Fm" + ima1 + ")/(GTc" + ima1 + "+Fm" + ima1 + "+1.1*e27)+0.9*e27"
            If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value = 2 Then
                PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + ZAP + ">aa004)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                Else
                
                ' проверяем не насос ли
                nas = 0
                'If InStr(ima2, "pip") > 0 Then nas = 1
                'If InStr(ima2, "pop") > 0 Then nas = 1
                If nas = 0 Then
                PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + "" + "10>aa004)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                Else
                PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + "" + "-10000>aa004)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                End If
                
                
            End If
        PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "{" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "n_max=" + Str(nTEK) + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

            If Cells(i + 3, 7).Value = 2 Then
                PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa004=" + ZAP + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                Else
                PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "aa004=810;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            End If
        PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "}" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Cells(i, 7).Value + "-" + Replace("dPL_r[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str((i0 + 2) / 7) + "]", " ", "") + ">pmax) pmax=" + Cells(i, 7).Value + "-" + Replace("dPL_r[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str((i0 + 2) / 7) + "]", " ", "") + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        PMAXMIN = PMAXMIN + "if(" + Cells(i, 7).Value + "-" + Replace("dPL_r[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str((i0 + 2) / 7) + "]", " ", "") + "<pmin) pmin=" + Cells(i, 7).Value + "-" + Replace("dPL_r[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str((i0 + 2) / 7) + "]", " ", "") + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

End Function


Sub Read_row(ii As Single)
If Cells(ii + 6, 5).Value = 2 Then
Call Read_row_2(ii)
Else
Call Read_row_1(ii)
End If

End Sub

Sub Read_row_1(ii As Single) '*dell
i = ii
' NEW CONDITION
kgs = 1 ' коэффициент гидравлического сопротивления линии
For j = 9 To 25 Step 2

imj = Cells(4, 4).Value
ims = Cells(i, 7).Value
dpj = "dPL_r[" + Str(Cells(4, 3).Value) + "," + Str(Cells(i, 6).Value) + "]"
zz1 = Cells(i, j).Value
zz2 = Cells(i + 1, j).Value
zz3 = Cells(i + 2, j).Value

iz1 = Cells(i, j + 1).Value
iz2 = Cells(i + 1, j + 1).Value
iz3 = Cells(i + 2, j + 1).Value


jjh = 0
'On Error Resume Next
If zz1 = "b" Or zz1 = "f" Then
a11 = 0
Else
aas = zz1
If IsNumeric(aas) = True Then
a11 = aas
Else
Cells(i, j).Value = 0
a11 = 0
End If
End If
If zz2 = "b" Or zz2 = "f" Then
a12 = 0
Else
aas = zz2
If IsNumeric(aas) = True Then
a12 = aas
Else
Cells(i + 1, j).Value = 0
a12 = 0
End If
End If
If zz3 = "b" Or zz3 = "f" Then
a13 = 0
Else
aas = zz3
If IsNumeric(aas) = True Then
a13 = aas
Else
Cells(i + 2, j).Value = 0
a13 = 0
End If
End If
If (a11 + a12 + a13) > 0 Then jjh = 1
If zz1 = "f" Or zz1 = "b" Then jjh = 1
If zz2 = "f" Or zz2 = "b" Then jjh = 1
If zz3 = "f" Or zz3 = "b" Then jjh = 1

If jjh = 1 Then
   If aa0 = 0 Then
aa1 = aa1 + "+(1/("
aa3 = aa3 + "+(1/("
aa0 = 1
   Else
aa1 = aa1 + "+1/("
aa3 = aa3 + "+1/("
   End If
     
      If Not (zz1) = "" Then ' строка 1 линии
   F0 = 0
   If zz1 = "f" Then F0 = 1
   If zz1 = "b" Then F0 = 2
     If F0 = 0 Then
     kgs = kgs * 1.3
     kgs = 1 ' исправил, убрать если нужен коэффициент !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!
aa1 = aa1 + Str(zz1) + "*Math.Pow(" + iz1 + ",2)"
aa3 = aa3 + Str(zz1) + "*Math.Pow(" + iz1 + ",2)"
     Else ' условие если есть обратный клапан
     'adou = adou + "double " + iz1 + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
     ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
     i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + iz1 + "_ok=0;"
         If F0 = 1 Then ' прямой клапан
         aa4 = aa4 + iz1 + "_ok=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
         aa4 = aa4 + " if (" + imj + "+" + dpj + "<" + ims + ") " + iz1 + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
         End If
         If F0 = 2 Then ' обратный клапан
         aa4 = aa4 + iz1 + "_ok=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
         aa4 = aa4 + " if (" + imj + "+" + dpj + ">" + ims + ") " + iz1 + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
         End If
         kgs = kgs * 1.3
         aa1 = aa1 + iz1 + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + iz1 + ",2)"
         aa3 = aa3 + iz1 + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + iz1 + ",2)"
     End If
   Else
aa1 = aa1 + "0"
aa3 = aa3 + "0"
   End If
   
    If Not (zz2) = "" Then ' строка 2 линии
   F0 = 0
   If zz2 = "f" Then F0 = 1
   If zz2 = "b" Then F0 = 2
   If F0 = 0 Then
   kgs = kgs * (1 + 0.5 * zz2)
   kgs = 1 ' исправил, убрать если нужен коэффициент !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!
aa1 = aa1 + "+" + Str(zz2) + "*Math.Pow(" + iz2 + ",2)"
aa3 = aa3 + "+" + Str(zz2) + "*Math.Pow(" + iz2 + ",2)"
     Else ' условие если есть обратный клапан
     'adou = adou + "double " + iz2 + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
          ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
     i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + iz2 + "_ok=0;"
         If F0 = 1 Then ' прямой клапан

         aa4 = aa4 + " if (" + imj + "+" + dpj + "<" + ims + ") " + iz2 + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

         aa1 = aa1 + "+" + iz2 + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + iz2 + ",2)"
         aa3 = aa3 + "+" + iz2 + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + iz2 + ",2)"
     End If
   End If
   
    If Not (zz3) = "" Then ' строка 3 линии

   If zz3 = "b" Then F0 = 2
   If F0 = 0 Then
   kgs = kgs * (1 + 0.5 * zz3)

         If F0 = 2 Then ' обратный клапан

         aa4 = aa4 + " if (" + imj + "+" + dpj + ">" + ims + ") " + iz3 + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
         End If
         kgs = kgs * (1 + 0.5 * 1)
         aa1 = aa1 + "+" + iz3 + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + iz3 + ",2)"
         aa3 = aa3 + "+" + iz3 + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + iz3 + ",2)"
     End If
   End If
aa1 = aa1 + "+0.01*e27)"
aa3 = aa3 + "+0.01*e27)"
End If
Next j

If Len(Cells(i, 28).Value) > 1 Then
 F0 = 0
   If Cells(i, 27).Value = "f" Then F0 = 1
   If Cells(i, 27).Value = "b" Then F0 = 2
   If F0 = 0 Then

Else ' условие если есть обратный клапан
     adou = adou + "double " + Cells(i, 28).Value + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            If F0 = 1 Then ' прямой клапан
            aa4 = aa4 + Cells(i, 28).Value + "_ok=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            aa4 = aa4 + " if (" + imj + "+" + dpj + "<" + ims + ") " + Cells(i, 28).Value + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            End If
            If F0 = 2 Then ' обратный клапан
            aa4 = aa4 + Cells(i, 28).Value + "_ok=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            aa4 = aa4 + " if (" + imj + "+" + dpj + ">" + ims + ") " + Cells(i, 28).Value + "_ok=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

         aa3 = aa3 + "+1/(" + Cells(i, 28).Value + "_ok" + "*Math.Pow(" + Cells(i, 28).Value + ",2)" + "+0.01*e27)"

Sub CG_start3() '*dell
3
method_nom = 2
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
df = Cells(i, 7).Value
df2 = Len(Cells(i, 7).Value)

dfl = Len(df)
Next i

m00 = Month(Date)
y00 = Year(Date)
uy = (y00 - 2024) * 12 + m00

'Дата стирания триала

uy0 = (del_y - 2024) * 12 + del_m

End If

End Sub

ato = 0
If Len(Cells(16, 3).Value) + Len(Cells(17, 3).Value) > 3 Then ato = 1
If ato = 1 Then 'значит считаем измененеи температуры
tnm = tnm + "=" + tnm + "+(" + Replace(Cells(16, 3).Value, " ", "") + ")/(0.00000000001+Math.Abs(" + Replace(Cells(17, 3).Value, " ", "") + "));"
Cells(18, 1).Value = Cells(18, 1).Value + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + " // Вносим изменения в температуру источника " + Replace(Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + tnm + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "if (" + tnm0 + ">2000) " + tnm0 + "=2000;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "if (" + tnm0 + "<0.1) " + tnm0 + "=0.1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If

'анализ признания источника двухфазным
Cells(72, 1).Value = 0 ' 1 - 2х фазный, 0 нет
GG = 0
LL = 0
verno = 0
For i = 10 To 136 Step 7
If Replace(Cells(i, 7).Value, " ", "") = "G" Then GG = 1
If Replace(Cells(i, 7).Value, " ", "") = "L" Then LL = 1
Next i
If GG + LL = 2 Then verno = 1
If GG + LL = 1 Then verno = 2
If verno = 2 Then
MsgBox ("На листе " + Cells(4, 4).Value + " указан выход только одной фазы из 2-х. Исправьте ошибку!")
End If
aOI = ""
If verno = 1 Then ' значит надо считать ОИ
Cells(72, 1).Value = 1
Cells(74, 1).Value = Cells(74, 1).Value + "double Em" + Cells(4, 4).Value + "=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) 'добавляем дабл долю отгона
Cells(74, 1).Value = Cells(74, 1).Value + "double Gm" + Cells(4, 4).Value + "=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) 'добавляем дабл расход газов
Cells(74, 1).Value = Cells(74, 1).Value + "double Lm" + Cells(4, 4).Value + "=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) 'добавляем дабл расход жидкости

' переменные состава (первые 5)
u1 = Replace(nmSv(1) + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "")
u2 = Replace(nmSv(2) + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "")
u3 = Replace(nmSv(3) + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "")
u4 = Replace(nmSv(4) + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "")
u5 = Replace(nmSv(5) + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "")


aOI = aOI + " " + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " // Расчёт доли отгона, состава и расходов фаз для " + Cells(4, 4).Value + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " tst = 0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " if (" + Replace("Tc" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + " > 600) tst = 1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " if (" + Replace("Tc" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + "< -120) tst = 1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " if (tst==0)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " {" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " OI(" + u1 + ", " + u2 + "," + u3 + "," + u4 + "," + u5 + "," + Replace("Tc" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + ", 1 +" + Replace(Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace("Gm" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + " = " + Replace("GTc" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + " * eG; // расход газов сверху" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace("Lm" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + " = " + Replace("GTc" + Cells(4, 4).Value, " ", "") + " * (1 - eG); // расход жидкости снизу" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " // состав газов (массовые доли)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u1 + "_G", " ", "") + " = yG[1];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u2 + "_G", " ", "") + " = yG[2];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u3 + "_G", " ", "") + " = yG[3];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u4 + "_G", " ", "") + " = yG[4];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u5 + "_G", " ", "") + " = yG[5];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " // состав жидкости (массовые доли)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u1 + "_L", " ", "") + " = xG[1];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u2 + "_L", " ", "") + " = xG[2];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u3 + "_L", " ", "") + " = xG[3];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u4 + "_L", " ", "") + " = xG[4];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " " + Replace(u5 + "_L", " ", "") + " = xG[5];" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
aOI = aOI + " }" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(18, 1).Value = Cells(18, 1).Value + aOI + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If

If TIPmb = 1 Then Worksheets("CODE").Cells(9, 1).Value = chMB
End Sub
